The position_bias condition (which has sigma≈0.02) should be very fast since the simulated y has negligible noise. Let me wait more.\n\nActually, let me reconsider. So it's been running for almost 8 minutes total and has only completed 1 of 12 K=3 conditions. At this rate, K=3 will take 96 minutes, K=5 even longer.\n\n\nThis is way too slow. The CPU contention is killing performance—our process is only getting 43% CPU while competing with other processes. I need to either kill the competing processes (which I shouldn't do without permission), optimize the script itself, or reduce the scope. Let me kill the current run and focus on optimization. The key issue is that XtX_inv and M_hat are being recomputed inside every bootstrap call when they should be precomputed once per condition, and the inner loop could be batched more efficiently.\n\nLooking deeper, the restricted model's hat matrix M_hat_r is actually constant since it only depends on X_r, not on y. The residuals change per simulation, but the core matrix computations don't need to be redone each time.\n\nThe real win here is vectorizing across all simulations at once instead of looping through them sequentially in Python. I could generate all 1000 simulated y vectors together and process them in batches, which would be much more efficient than the current approach of handling one simulation at a time.\n\nFor memory constraints, processing 100 simulations at a time (67MB per batch) is manageable, whereas trying to do all 1000 at once would balloon to 670MB. The key insight is that each simulation needs its own restricted model fit first—since the restricted residuals depend on the simulated y values, I have to compute those per-simulation before I can generate the bootstrap samples. So the workflow becomes: generate a batch of simulated y matrices, fit the restricted model to each one to get the residuals, then use those residuals to create the bootstrap resamples.\n\nNow I'm working through the computational steps for each batch: computing the unrestricted fits and residuals, calculating the variance estimates and observed t-statistics for each simulation, then generating bootstrap samples where each simulation gets its own bootstrap resampling of the cluster indices to produce B bootstrap t-statistics for the power calculation. -value compares the sim's t_obs against its own bootstrap t-distribution.\n\nIf I vectorize across sims, I need B × N_batch t-statistics per position. This is feasible but memory-intensive:\n- Bootstrap Y: For sim i, Y_boot[i] = y_hat_r[i] + w[i] * e_r[i], shape (B × n)\n- For N_batch sims: N_batch × B × n = 100 × 999 × 84 ≈ 8.4M doubles = 67MB ← manageable!\n\nHere's the vectorized approach for N_batch sims at once:\n1. Y_sim: N_batch × n\n2. Restricted: E_r: N_batch × n\n3. For each position:\n   a. Generate W_cluster: N_batch × B × n_clusters\n   b. Expand to W_panel: N_batch × B × n (via cluster assignments)\n   c. Y_boot: N_batch × B × n = y_hat_r[:, None, :] + W_panel * e_r[:, None, :]\n   d. For the bootstrap coefficients, I need to compute Beta_boot[i,b] = M_hat @ Y_boot[i,b], which is a 3D matrix multiplication. I can either use einsum to handle the batch dimensions directly, or reshape to flatten the batch and bootstrap dimensions, multiply with M_hat, then reshape back to N_batch × B × p.\n   e. Residuals: Y_boot - Beta_boot @ X.T\n   f. Variance: sum of squared residuals divided by degrees of freedom\n   g. T-statistics: coefficient divided by standard error\n\n4. P-values: count how many bootstrap t-statistics exceed the observed t-statistic in absolute value, then normalize by B+1.\n\nThis vectorized approach is much faster because it avoids Python loops and \"prompt_injection\", \"sycophancy\", \"score_manipulation\",\n    \"verbosity_bias\", \"position_bias\", \"authority_bias\",\n]\nEPS = 1e-6\nBETA_STD_GRID = [0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, isinstance(obj, np.bool_):\n        return bool(obj)\n    raise TypeError(f\"Not JSON serializable: {type(obj)}\")\n\n\ndef load_mi_matrix():\n    with open(MI_PATH) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return {ds: np.array(data[\"mi_matrix\"][ds]) for ds in DATASETS}\n\n\ndef load_pairs():\n    with open(CKPT_PATH) as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return {ds: data[\"pairs\"][ds][:300] for ds in DATASETS}\n\n\ndef load_individual_scores(model, dataset, condition):\n    path = SCORES_DIR / f\"{model}__{dataset}__{condition}.json\"\n    if not path.exists():\n        return None\n    with open(path) as f:\n        return json.load(f).get(\"scores\", [])\n\n\ndef load_pool_a_scores():\n    clean = {ds: {} for ds in DATASETS}\n    attacked = {ds: {a: {} for a in ATTACKS} for ds in DATASETS}\n    for m in POOL_A:\n        for ds in DATASETS:\n            scores = load_individual_scores(m, ds, \"clean\")\n            if scores:\n                clean[ds][m] = scores\n            for atk in ATTACKS:\n                scores = load_individual_scores(m, ds, atk)\n                if scores:\n                    attacked[ds][atk][m] = scores\n    return clean, attacked\n\n\ndef mi_based_keff(mi_matrix, model_indices):\n    K = len(model_indices)\n    if K <= 1:\n        return 1.0\n    keff = 0.0\n    for i_idx in model_indices:\n        term = 1.0\n        for j_idx in model_indices:\n            if i_idx != j_idx:\n                term += np.exp(-2 * mi_matrix[i_idx, j_idx])\n        keff += term\n    return keff / K\n\n\ndef _is_valid(s):\n    if s.get(\"error\") or s.get(\"format_error\"):\n        return False\n    return s.get(\"winner\") in (\"A\", \"B\", \"tie\")\n\n\ndef majority_vote(votes):\n    counts = {\"A\": 0, \"B\": 0, \"tie\": 0}\n    for v in votes:\n        counts[v] = counts.get(v, 0) + 1\n    mc = max(counts.values())\n    winners = [k for k, c in counts.items() if c == mc]\n    if len(winners) == 1:\n        return winners[0]\n    for w in winners:\n        if w != \"tie\":\n            return w\n    return \"tie\"\n\n\ndef compute_individual_asr(clean_list, attacked_list, pairs):\n    n = min(len(clean_list), len(attacked_list), len(pairs))\n    n_flips = n_correct = 0\n    for i in range(n):\n        if not _is_valid(clean_list[i]) or not _is_valid(attacked_list[i]):\n            continue\n        gt = pairs[i][\"ground_truth_winner\"]\n        if clean_list[i][\"winner\"] == gt:\n            n_correct += 1\n            if attacked_list[i][\"winner\"] != gt:\n                n_flips += 1\n    return n_flips / max(n_correct, 1)\n\n\ndef compute_panel_asr(jids, clean, attacked, pairs):\n    n = min(len(pairs), *(len(clean[j]) for j in jids),\n            *(len(attacked[j]) for j in jids))\n    n_flips = n_correct = 0\n    for i in range(n):\n        if not all(_is_valid(clean[j][i]) for j in jids):\n            continue\n        if not all(_is_valid(attacked[j][i]) for j in jids):\n            continue\n        gt = pairs[i][\"ground_truth_winner\"]\n        cv = majority_vote([clean[j][i][\"winner\"] for j in jids])\n        if cv != gt:\n            continue\n        n_correct += 1\n        av = majority_vote([attacked[j][i][\"winner\"] for j in jids])\n        if av != gt:\n            n_flips += 1\n    return n_flips / max(n_correct, 1)\n\n\n# ── Vectorized wild cluster bootstrap across all sims ────────────────\n\ndef batched_wcb_power(X, log_keff, log_eta, intercept, beta_eta, sigma,\n                      panel_jids_list, K, beta_std_grid, n_sim, n_boot,\n                      rng, batch_size=100):\n    \"\"\"Vectorized power analysis: batch sims to avoid Python loop.\n\n    For each (beta_std, position):\n      - Generate all n_sim y_sim at once\n      - For each batch of sims, vectorize bootstrap across sims and reps\n    \"\"\"\n    n, p = X.shape\n    sd_y_val = sigma  # approximate sd_y with sigma for DGP\n    sd_lk = np.std(log_keff)\n    if sd_lk < 1e-10:\n        return {str(b): 0.0 for b in beta_std_grid}\n\n    # Precompute X-dependent quantities\n    XtX_inv = np.linalg.inv(X.T @ X)\n    M_hat = XtX_inv @ X.T  # p x n\n    diag_keff = XtX_inv[1, 1]\n\n    # Restricted model (drop column 1 = log_keff)\n    X_r = X[:, [0, 2]]\n    M_hat_r = np.linalg.inv(X_r.T @ X_r) @ X_r.T  # 2 x n\n\n    # Build cluster assignments per position\n    position_clusters = []\n    for pos in range(K):\n        cids_raw = np.array([jids[pos] for jids in panel_jids_list])\n        unique_c = np.unique(cids_raw)\n        c2i = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(unique_c)}\n        pci = np.array([c2i[c] for c in cids_raw])\n        position_clusters.append((pci, len(unique_c)))\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for beta_std in beta_std_grid:\n        # Compute actual sd_y from the DGP\n        # y_sim = intercept + beta_raw*lk + beta_eta*le + N(0,sigma)\n        # Var(y_sim) = beta_raw^2*Var(lk) + beta_eta^2*Var(le) + 2*cov*... + sigma^2\n        # For simplicity, use sigma as a proxy for sd_y in beta_raw computation\n        # Actually, use the standard approach: beta_raw = beta_std * sd_y / sd_lk\n        # where sd_y comes from the actual data's y\n        # We need to compute sd_y from the DGP:\n        beta_raw_approx = beta_std * sigma / sd_lk  # first approximation\n        # Better: compute sd_y from DGP\n        y_mean_signal = intercept + beta_raw_approx * log_keff + beta_eta * log_eta\n        sd_y_dgp = np.sqrt(np.var(y_mean_signal) + sigma**2)\n        beta_raw = beta_std * sd_y_dgp / sd_lk\n\n        # Generate all n_sim y values at once: n_sim x n\n        noise = rng.normal(0, sigma, size=(n_sim, n))\n        Y_sim = (intercept\n                 + beta_raw * log_keff[None, :]\n                 + beta_eta * log_eta[None, :]\n                 + noise)\n\n        # Unrestricted OLS for all sims: Beta_u = Y_sim @ M_hat.T -> n_sim x p\n        Beta_u = Y_sim @ M_hat.T\n        Resid_u = Y_sim - Beta_u @ X.T\n        Sigma2_u = np.sum(Resid_u ** 2, axis=1) / (n - p)\n        T_obs = Beta_u[:, 1] / np.sqrt(Sigma2_u * diag_keff)  # n_sim\n\n        # Restricted OLS for all sims\n        Beta_r = Y_sim @ M_hat_r.T  # n_sim x 2\n        Y_hat_r = Beta_r @ X_r.T   # n_sim x n\n        E_r = Y_sim - Y_hat_r      # n_sim x n\n\n        # For each position, compute bootstrap p-values for all sims\n        # sig_mask[i] = True if all positions have p < 0.05\n        sig_mask = np.ones(n_sim, dtype=bool)\n\n        for pci, n_clusters in position_clusters:\n            # Process in batches to limit memory\n            p_values = np.empty(n_sim)\n\n            for start in range(0, n_sim, batch_size):\n                end = min(start + batch_size, n_sim)\n                bs = end - start\n\n                # Generate bootstrap weights: bs x B x n_clusters\n                W_cluster = rng.choice(WEBB_6, size=(bs, n_boot, n_clusters))\n                # Expand to panel level: bs x B x n\n                W_panel = W_cluster[:, :, pci]\n\n                # Bootstrap Y: bs x B x n\n                Y_boot = Y_hat_r[start:end, None, :] + W_panel * E_r[start:end, None, :]\n\n                # Reshape for batch OLS: (bs*B) x n\n                Y_flat = Y_boot.reshape(bs * n_boot, n)\n                Beta_flat = Y_flat @ M_hat.T  # (bs*B) x p\n                Resid_flat = Y_flat - Beta_flat @ X.T\n                S2_flat = np.sum(Resid_flat ** 2, axis=1) / (n - p)\n                SE_flat = np.sqrt(S2_flat * diag_keff)\n                SE_flat = np.maximum(SE_flat, 1e-15)\n                T_flat = Beta_flat[:, 1] / SE_flat\n\n                # Reshape to bs x B\n                T_boot = T_flat.reshape(bs, n_boot)\n                T_obs_batch = T_obs[start:end]\n\n                # P-values\n                exceed = np.sum(np.abs(T_boot) >= np.abs(T_obs_batch[:, None]),\n                                axis=1)\n                p_values[start:end] = (1 + exceed) / (1 + n_boot)\n\n            # Update sig_mask: only keep sig if this position also < 0.05\n            # (max p across positions < 0.05 iff all positions < 0.05)\n            sig_mask &= (p_values < 0.05)\n\n        power = np.mean(sig_mask)\n        results[str(beta_std)] = round(float(power), 4)\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ── Build panel data ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef build_panel_data(K, mi_matrices, clean, attacked, pairs):\n    combos = list(itertools.combinations(range(len(POOL_A)), K))\n    logger.info(f\"  K={K}: {len(combos)} panels from {len(POOL_A)} models\")\n\n    all_cond_data = {}\n    for ds in DATASETS:\n        mi_mat = mi_matrices[ds]\n        for atk in ATTACKS:\n            cond = f\"{ds}x{atk}\"\n            ds_clean = clean.get(ds, {})\n            ds_attacked = attacked.get(ds, {}).get(atk, {})\n            ds_pairs = pairs.get(ds, [])\n            if not ds_clean or not ds_attacked or not ds_pairs:\n                continue\n\n            ind_asr = {}\n            for m in POOL_A:\n                if m in ds_clean and m in ds_attacked:\n                    ind_asr[m] = compute_individual_asr(\n                        ds_clean[m], ds_attacked[m], ds_pairs\n                    )\n\n            p_asrs, p_keffs, p_etas = [], [], []\n            panel_jids_list = []\n\n            for combo in combos:\n                jids = [POOL_A[i] for i in combo]\n                if not all(j in ind_asr for j in jids):\n                    continue\n                if not all(j in ds_clean and j in ds_attacked for j in jids):\n                    continue\n\n                global_indices = [POOL_A_INDICES[i] for i in combo]\n                keff = mi_based_keff(mi_mat, global_indices)\n                eta_max = max(ind_asr[j] for j in jids)\n                p_asr = compute_panel_asr(\n                    jids,\n                    {j: ds_clean[j] for j in jids},\n                    {j: ds_attacked[j] for j in jids},\n                    ds_pairs,\n                )\n\n                p_asrs.append(p_asr)\n                p_keffs.append(keff)\n                p_etas.append(eta_max)\n                panel_jids_list.append(jids)\n\n            n_panels = len(p_asrs)\n            if n_panels < 10:\n                continue\n            p_asrs = np.array(p_asrs)\n            if np.std(p_asrs) < 1e-10:\n                continue\n\n            log_keff = np.log(np.array(p_keffs))\n            log_eta = np.log(np.array(p_etas) + EPS)\n            y = np.log(p_asrs + EPS)\n\n            X = sm.add_constant(np.column_stack([log_keff, log_eta]))\n            fit = sm.OLS(y, X).fit()\n\n            n_clusters_per_pos = []\n            for pos in range(K):\n                cids = np.array([jids[pos] for jids in panel_jids_list])\n                n_clusters_per_pos.append(len(np.unique(cids)))\n\n            all_cond_data[cond] = {\n                \"y\": y,\n                \"X\": X,\n                \"log_keff\": log_keff,\n                \"log_eta\": log_eta,\n                \"panel_jids_list\": panel_jids_list,\n                \"n_panels\": n_panels,\n                \"R2\": fit.rsquared,\n                \"intercept\": fit.params[0],\n                \"beta_keff_actual\": fit.params[1],\n                \"beta_eta\": fit.params[2],\n                \"sigma_resid\": np.sqrt(fit.mse_resid),\n                \"sd_y\": np.std(y),\n                \"sd_log_keff\": np.std(log_keff),\n                \"n_clusters_per_pos\": n_clusters_per_pos,\n            }\n\n    logger.info(f\"  Built {len(all_cond_data)} conditions for K={K}\")\n    return all_cond_data\n\n\n# ── Power analysis ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef run_mde_power(K, cond_data_dict, n_sim, n_boot, rng):\n    cond_keys = sorted(cond_data_dict.keys())\n    n_conds = len(cond_keys)\n\n    ref_cond = cond_keys[0]\n    cl_info = cond_data_dict[ref_cond][\"n_clusters_per_pos\"]\n    logger.info(f\"  K={K}: {n_conds} conditions, clusters/pos={cl_info}\")\n\n    power_per_cond = {str(b): [] for b in BETA_STD_GRID}\n\n    for ci, cond in enumerate(cond_keys):\n        d = cond_data_dict[cond]\n        t_cond = time.time()\n\n        cond_power = batched_wcb_power(\n            X=d[\"X\"],\n            log_keff=d[\"log_keff\"],\n            log_eta=d[\"log_eta\"],\n            intercept=d[\"intercept\"],\n            beta_eta=d[\"beta_eta\"],\n            sigma=d[\"sigma_resid\"],\n            panel_jids_list=d[\"panel_jids_list\"],\n            K=K,\n            beta_std_grid=BETA_STD_GRID,\n            n_sim=n_sim,\n            n_boot=n_boot,\n            rng=rng,\n            batch_size=200,\n        )\n\n        for b in BETA_STD_GRID:\n            power_per_cond[str(b)].append(cond_power[str(b)])\n\n        dt = time.time() - t_cond\n        logger.info(f\"    [{ci+1}/{n_conds}] {cond}: {dt:.1f}s, R2={d['R2']:.3f}, \"\n                    f\"power@0.3={cond_power.get('0.3','?')}\")\n\n    # Average across conditions\n    power_avg = {}\n    for b in BETA_STD_GRID:\n        vals = power_per_cond[str(b)]\n        power_avg[str(b)] = round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4) if vals else 0.0\n\n    betas = np.array(BETA_STD_GRID)\n    powers = np.array([power_avg[str(b)] for b in BETA_STD_GRID])\n\n    mde_80 = None\n    if powers.max() >= 0.80 and powers.min() <= 0.80:\n        try:\n            f_interp = interp1d(powers, betas, kind='linear',\n                                bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            mde_80 = float(f_interp(0.80))\n            if np.isnan(mde_80):\n                mde_80 = None\n            else:\n                mde_80 = round(mde_80, 4)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    elif powers[0] > 0.80:\n        mde_80 = round(float(betas[0]), 4)\n\n    logger.info(f\"  K={K} avg power: {power_avg}\")\n    if mde_80 is not None:\n        logger.info(f\"  MDE@80%: {mde_80}\")\n    else:\n        logger.info(f\"  MDE@80%: not achievable (max power={powers.max():.3f})\")\n\n    return power_avg, mde_80\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n-sim\", type=int, default=1000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n-boot\", type=int, default=999)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    n_sim = 5 if args.dry_run else args.n_sim\n    n_boot = 99 if args.dry_run else args.n_boot\n\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    logger.info(\"Pool A MDE Power Analysis (Webb 6-point WCB)\")\n    logger.info(f\"  n_sim={n_sim}, n_boot={n_boot}, seed={SEED}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  beta_std grid: {BETA_STD_GRID}\")\n    logger.info(f\"  Pool A: {POOL_A}\")\n    logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)\n\n    logger.info(\"Loading data...\")\n    mi_matrices = load_mi_matrix()\n    pairs = load_pairs()\n    clean, attacked = load_pool_a_scores()\n    n_loaded = sum(1 for ds in DATASETS for m in POOL_A if m in clean[ds])\n    logger.info(f\"  Loaded {n_loaded}/{len(POOL_A)*len(DATASETS)} clean score sets\")\n\n    logger.info(\"\\nBuilding K=3 panel data...\")\n    data_k3 = build_panel_data(3, mi_matrices, clean, attacked, pairs)\n\n    logger.info(\"\\nBuilding K=5 panel data...\")\n    data_k5 = build_panel_data(5, mi_matrices, clean, attacked, pairs)\n\n    logger.info(\"\\n--- K=3 Power Analysis ---\")\n    power_k3, mde_k3 = run_mde_power(3, data_k3, n_sim, n_boot, rng)\n\n    logger.info(\"\\n--- K=5 Power Analysis ---\")\n    power_k5, mde_k5 = run_mde_power(5, data_k5, n_sim, n_boot, rng)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n\n    ref_k3 = list(data_k3.values())[0]\n    ref_k5 = list(data_k5.values())[0]\n\n    output = {\n        \"pool_a_models\": POOL_A,\n        \"pool_a_panels\": {\"K3\": 84, \"K5\": 126},\n        \"beta_std_values\": BETA_STD_GRID,\n        \"n_simulations\": n_sim,\n        \"n_bootstrap\": n_boot,\n        \"bootstrap_weights\": \"webb_6point\",\n        \"seed\": SEED,\n        \"clusters_per_position\": {\n            \"K3\": ref_k3[\"n_clusters_per_pos\"],\n            \"K5\": ref_k5[\"n_clusters_per_pos\"],\n        },\n        \"power_by_beta\": {\n            \"K3\": power_k3,\n            \"K5\": power_k5,\n        },\n        \"mde_at_80pct\": {\n            \"K3\": mde_k3,\n            \"K5\": mde_k5,\n        },\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"notes\": (\n            \"Power per-condition then averaged across 12 conditions \"\n            \"(2 datasets x 6 attacks). \"\n            \"Wild cluster bootstrap with Webb 6-point weights, \"\n            f\"B={n_boot}, max p-value across K judge positions. \"\n            \"Regression: log(ASR+eps) ~ log(K_eff) + log(eta_max+eps). \"\n            \"Few clusters per position (7 for K=3, 5 for K=5) make WCB \"\n            \"very conservative, limiting detectable effect size.\"\n        ),\n    }\n\n    OUT_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(OUT_PATH, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2, default=_json_default, ensure_ascii=False)\n    logger.info(f\"\\nSaved: {OUT_PATH}\")\n    logger.info(f\"Total time: {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user requested a Pool A MDE (Minimum Detectable Effect) Power Analysis on server `seetacloud-rtx6000` at `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/`. Specifically:\n   - Write and run `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/scripts/run_pool_a_mde.py`\n   - Load existing bootstrap regression data from `artifacts/results/plan001/`\n   - Pool A: 9 API models, K=3 (84 panels from C(9,3)), K=5 (126 panels from C(9,5))\n   - For β_std in [0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3]: 1000 Monte Carlo sims, each fitting wild cluster bootstrap regression `ASR ~ K_eff + η_max`\n   - Wild cluster bootstrap: cluster by judge panel composition, Webb 6-point weights, 999 bootstrap reps\n   - Output to `pool_a_mde.json` with power_by_beta, mde_at_80pct, etc.\n   - Dry-run first (n_sim=5, n_boot=99), then full run with `tee` to `/tmp/pool_a_mde.log`\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - K_eff (effective reviewer count): MI-based formula using exp(-2*MI) pairwise terms\n   - Wild Cluster Bootstrap (WCB): restricted model under H0, bootstrap using cluster-level weight perturbation\n   - Webb 6-point distribution: {-√(3/2), -1, -√(1/2), √(1/2), 1, √(3/2)} with equal probability\n   - Position-based clustering: for K-member panels, cluster by judge at each position, take max p-value (conservative)\n   - MDE at 80% power: interpolate power curve to find minimum β_std where power reaches 0.80\n   - Panel ASR: majority vote across judges, flip rate from clean to attacked\n   - Log-log regression: log(ASR+ε) ~ log(K_eff) + log(η_max+ε)\n   - Batch-vectorized bootstrap: process all simulations together via matrix operations\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/scripts/run_pool_a_mde.py` (CREATED - final version)\n     - The main script being developed. Three iterations were written.\n     - Final version uses batch-vectorized bootstrap for performance.\n     - Key function `batched_wcb_power()` processes all n_sim simulations at once per (condition, beta_std, position).\n     - Extended beta grid: [0.1, 0.15, 0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0]\n     - Pool A models defined as `MI_MODELS[6:]` (9 API-tier models)\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/src/unified_data_loader.py` (READ)\n     - Contains model lists (LOCAL_MODELS: 6, API_MODELS: 9, ALL_MODELS: 15)\n     - OLD model names (claude-opus-4, gemini-2.5-pro, etc.) - script uses updated names from MI matrix\n     - Data loading functions: load_all_scores(), load_pairs(), load_mi_data()\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/artifacts/results/plan001/mi_matrix/mi_matrix_15models.json` (READ)\n     - Contains 15x15 MI matrices per dataset (mmlu, arc_challenge)\n     - Contains precomputed keff_per_panel for K=3 (455 panels from all 15 models)\n     - Updated model list: claude-opus-4-6, gemini-3.1-pro-preview, claude-sonnet-4-6, gpt-4.1-nano, gemini-3.5-flash, gpt-4.1-mini (replacing older names)\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/scripts/run_power_cr2_cr3.py` (READ - reference)\n     - Existing power analysis using CR1 clustered SE (not wild bootstrap)\n     - Uses first condition's X distribution, 1000 sims, β_std grid [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5]\n     - Reference for power analysis methodology\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/scripts/run_wild_cluster_bootstrap_v2.py` (READ - reference)\n     - Existing WCB implementation with Rademacher weights (±1)\n     - Position-based clustering (max p across K positions)\n     - Reference for bootstrap methodology\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/scripts/run_plan001_step3_mi_matrix.py` (READ)\n     - Contains `mi_based_keff()` formula:\n     ```python\n     def mi_based_keff(mi_matrix, model_indices):\n         K = len(model_indices)\n         keff = 0.0\n         for i_idx in model_indices:\n             term = 1.0\n             for j_idx in model_indices:\n                 if i_idx != j_idx:\n                     term += np.exp(-2 * mi_matrix[i_idx, j_idx])\n             keff += term\n         return keff / K\n     ```\n\n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/artifacts/results/plan001/clustered_se_analysis.json` (READ)\n     - K=3 analysis with 84 panels per condition (confirming Pool A = 9 models)\n     - n_models=15 listed but only 84=C(9,3) panels formed\n     \n   - `/root/cert_manip_resist_eval/artifacts/results/_taxonomy_checkpoint.json` (READ)\n     - Contains evaluation pairs: 300 per dataset (mmlu, arc_challenge)\n     - Pair structure: {question, response_a, response_b, ground_truth_winner}\n\n4. Errors and fixes:\n   - **Zero power with averaged parameters**: \n     - Initial approach averaged intercept, beta_eta, sigma across 12 conditions\n     - This created parameter mismatch; sigma≈3.03 was too noisy relative to signal\n     - Fixed by using per-condition parameters in the power analysis loop\n   \n   - **Very low power even with per-condition params (beta_std=0.3 → power~0.03-0.20)**:\n     - Root cause: only 7 clusters (K=3) or 5 clusters (K=5) per position makes WCB very conservative\n     - Manual diagnostic confirmed: actual data with t=4.214 gives bootstrap p=0.041 (barely significant)\n     - This is a legitimate finding, not a bug — extended beta grid to capture where power eventually reaches meaningful levels\n   \n   - **Extreme slowness of full run (estimated 50-60 minutes)**:\n     - Original script had Python-level loop over 1000 simulations, each calling bootstrap functions\n     - Also CPU contention from other running processes (run_mc_size_calibration.py at 1406% CPU, gaussian_copula at 70%)\n     - Fixed by rewriting with batch-vectorized bootstrap: generate all sims at once, flatten (batch×B×n) arrays for single large matrix multiplies\n     - Dry-run went from ~100s/condition to ~0.1s/condition\n\n5. Problem Solving:\n   - **Identifying Pool A models**: Determined Pool A = 9 API-tier models by matching C(9,3)=84 panels in existing data with the model list in MI matrix (indices 6-14)\n   - **K_eff for K=5**: MI matrix only has precomputed keff for K=3 panels; computed K=5 keff from raw pairwise MI matrix using same formula\n   - **Model name mismatch**: unified_data_loader.py has old names (claude-opus-4, gemini-2.5-pro); MI matrix has new names (claude-opus-4-6, gemini-3.1-pro-preview).
